```json
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",
```

```text
- **Lift**: +28.3pp over majority baseline

- **Checkpoint**: `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/qwen15b_exception_only_seed42/final/`

This is remarkable—the 1.5B model matches the 7B model's performance at 99.83% accuracy with exception_only training, which really validates that exception events are the critical signal for this task.

Comparing across models, I'm seeing consistent results: both Qwen variants hit 99.83% with exceptions, while DeepSeek shows more variance depending on the seed. The exception signal is clearly robust enough that model size doesn't matter much once you have the right training data.

Now I need to update the registry with these results, refresh the report, figure out what to do with the freed GPU, notify the Director, and check what's next in the literature map.

(Google DeepMind)\\\"\\n        year: 2024\\n        venue: \\\"ICML 2024\\\"\\n        role: method-component\\n        key_finding: \\\"自训练在 execution trace 上做 CoT rationale，程序修复 fix rate 在 MBPP +26.1%、HumanEval +14.3%。模型可泛化到无 trace 的场景。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          mbpp_fix_rate_improvement: \\\"+26.1%\\\"\\n          humaneval_fix_rate_improvement: \\\"+14.3%\\\"\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Do Code Semantics Help? A Comprehensive Study on Execution Trace-Based Information for Code LLMs\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Jian Wang, Xiaofei Xie, Qiang Hu, Shangqing Liu, Yi Li\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"Findings of EMNLP 2025\\\"\\n        arxiv_id: \\\"2509.11686\\\"\\n        role: direct-competitor\\n        key_finding: \\\"综合研究发现 execution trace 对 SFT 和 test-time scaling 的帮助有限，与先前工作结论相左。提出 trace adapter 做格式化。这是 VOTA 必须回应的关键负面结果——说明原始 trace 不够，需要针对性压缩/抽象。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Code Execution as Grounded Supervision for LLM Reasoning\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Dongwon Jung, Wenxuan Zhou, Muhao Chen\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"EMNLP 2025\\\"\\n        arxiv_id: \\\"2506.10343\\\"\\n        role: method-component\\n        key_finding: \\\"用 Python debugger (Snoop) 提取 step-by-step execution trace，转换为自然语言 CoT。训练后模型推理能力提升且生成更少 token（减少 overthinking），具备跨域迁移能力。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\n      - title: \\\"What I cannot execute, I do not understand: Training and Evaluating LLMs on Program Execution Traces\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Lorenzo Ferrando, Nicola Ferraro, et al.\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"arXiv 2503.05703\\\"\\n        arxiv_id: \\\"2503.05703\\\"\\n        role: method-component\\n        key_finding: \\\"对比 regular/compact/dynamic 三种 scratchpad 策略，compact（仅记录变化变量）略优于 regular（全部变量值）。CruxEval/MBPP ~80%。直接支持 plan_001：完整值信息冗余，精简不损失效果。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          cruxeval_accuracy: \\\"~80%\\\"\\n          mbpp_accuracy: \\\"~80%\\\"\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Demystifying Errors in LLM Reasoning Traces: An Empirical Study of Code Execution Simulation\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Mohammad Abdollahi, Khandaker Rifah Tasnia, Soumit Kanti Saha, Jinqiu Yang, Song Wang, Hadi Hemmati\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"arXiv\\\"\\n        arxiv_id: \\\"2512.00215\\\"\\n        role: evaluation\\n        key_finding: \\\"LLM 在循环中频繁遗漏/多算变量修改，函数返回值预测不准。说明 LLM 对具体值追踪能力弱于结构理解，为 plan_001 发现（值冗余）提供机制解释。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Reasoning Runtime Behavior of a Program with LLM: How Far Are We? (REval)\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Junkai Chen et al.\\\"\\n        year: 2024\\n        venue: \\\"arXiv 2403.16437\\\"\\n        role: evaluation\\n        key_finding: \\\"REval 框架评估 LLM 在 4 种 runtime behavior 预测任务上的表现：coverage prediction、program state prediction、execution path prediction、output prediction。平均 accuracy 仅 44.4%，IC score 10.3。揭示 LLM runtime reasoning 能力有限，支持 VOTA 的发现——需要抽象而非让 LLM 直接消化全量 trace。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          avg_accuracy: \\\"44.4%\\\"\\n          avg_ic_score: \\\"10.3\\\"\\n\\n      - title: \\\"CodeMind: Evaluating Large Language Models for Code Reasoning\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Changshu Liu et al. (UIUC)\\\"\\n        year: 2024\\n        venue: \\\"arXiv 2402.09664\\\"\\n        role: evaluation\\n        key_finding: \\\"评估 LLM 在 IER（执行推理）、SR（规格推理）、DSR（动态语义推理）三个维度的代码推理能力。性能随代码复杂度（控制流、非原始类型、API 调用）显著下降。支持 VOTA 的 trace abstraction 动机——复杂 trace 超出 LLM 推理能力。\\\"\\n        metrics: {}\\n\\n      - title: \\\"CRUXEval-X: A Benchmark for Multilingual Code Reasoning, Understanding and Execution\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Ruiyang Xu et al. (HKUST)\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"ACL 2025\\\"\\n        arxiv_id: \\\"2408.13001\\\"\\n        role: evaluation\\n        key_finding: \\\"将 CRUXEval 扩展到 19 种编程语言，19K 测试。仅在 Python 训练的模型在其他语言最高 34.4% Pass@1，揭示 cross-language generalization 的局限。对 VOTA 的 cross-model generalization 实验提供方法论参考。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          languages: 19\\n          cross_lang_max_pass1: \\\"34.4%\\\"\\n\\n      - title: \\\"Chain of Execution Supervision Promotes General Reasoning in Large Language Models\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Nuo Chen, Zehua Li, Keqin Bao, Junyang Lin, Dayiheng Liu (Qwen/Alibaba, HKUST-GZ, USTC)\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"NeurIPS 2025\\\"\\n        arxiv_id: \\\"2510.23629\\\"\\n        verified: true\\n        role: direct-competitor\\n        key_finding: \\\"提出 TracePile——2.6M Chain-of-Execution trace 样本（~19B tokens），涵盖数学/算法/竞赛代码。在 4 个 base model、20 个 benchmark 上验证 trace 监督提升推理能力，但仅评估生成任务，未涉及 verdict classification。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          dataset_size: \\\"2.6M samples\\\"\\n          dataset_tokens: \\\"~19B\\\"\\n          benchmarks_evaluated: 20\\n\\n      - title: \\\"TRACED: Execution-aware Pre-training for Source Code\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Yangruibo Ding, Ben Steenhoek, Kexin Pei, Gail Kaiser, Wei Le, Baishakhi Ray\\\"\\n        year: 2024\\n        venue: \\\"ICSE 2024\\\"\\n        arxiv_id: \\\"2306.07487\\\"\\n        verified: true\\n        role: direct-competitor\\n        key_finding: \\\"用源码+可执行输入+execution trace 做 execution-aware 预训练（encoder-based），在执行路径预测提升 12.4%、运行时变量值预测提升 25.2%。证明 trace 有助于 encoder-based 分类任务，但仅用于预训练而非直接做 verdict classification。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          execution_path_prediction_improvement: \\\"+12.4%\\\"\\n          runtime_value_prediction_improvement: \\\"+25.2%\\\"\\n\\n  code-verdict:\\n    description: \\\"LLM 判断代码 pass/fail、正确性分类、mutation testing\\\"\\n    papers:\\n      - title: \\\"APPT: Boosting Automated Patch Correctness Prediction via Fine-tuning Pre-trained Models\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"iSEngLab (Nanjing University)\\\"\\n        year: 2024\\n        venue: \\\"IEEE TSE 2024\\\"\\n        role: direct-competitor\\n        key_finding: \\\"BERT + LSTM + classifier 微调做 patch 正确性二分类。在 1183 个 Defects4J patches 上 accuracy 79.7%、recall 83.2%，优于 CACHE。与 VOTA 的 verdict 分类目标最接近。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          accuracy: \\\"79.7%\\\"\\n          recall: \\\"83.2%\\\"\\n          f1_improvement_from_finetuning: \\\"+10.22%\\\"\\n\\n      - title: \\\"LLM4PatchCorrect: Leveraging Large Language Model for Automatic Patch Correctness Assessment\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Xin Zhou et al. (SMU)\\\"\\n        year: 2024\\n        venue: \\\"IEEE TSE 2024\\\"\\n        role: direct-competitor\\n  \\n\\n... title: \\\"Chain of Execution Supervision Promotes General Reasoning in Large Language Models\\\"\\n        authors: \\\"Nuo Chen, Zehua Li, Keqin Bao, Junyang Lin, Dayiheng Liu (Qwen/Alibaba, HKUST-GZ, USTC)\\\"\\n        year: 2025\\n        venue: \\\"NeurIPS 2025\\\"\\n        arxiv_id: \\\"2510.23629\\\"\\n        verified: true\\n        role: direct-competitor\\n        key_finding: \\\"提出 TracePile——2.6M Chain-of-Execution trace 样本（~19B tokens），涵盖数学/算法/竞赛代码。在 4 个 base model、20 个 benchmark 上验证 trace 监督提升推理能力，但仅评估生成任务，未涉及 verdict classification。\\\"\\n        metrics:\\n          dataset_size: \\\"2.6M samples\\\"\\n          dataset_tokens: \\\"~19B\\\"\\n          benchmarks_evaluated: 20\\n\\n      - {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/memory.md\", \"content\": \"APPEND_SECTION\\n\\n## DeepSeek eval max_length 不匹配教训 [2026-05-16]\\n\\n### 根因\\n- 训练 max_length=2048（train_sft.py L164/L193），94.1% 的 full_trace 样本被截断\\n- 原始 eval 用默认 max_length=2048 → 79.16%（正确，匹配训练）\\n- `run_missing_predictions.sh` 用 --max_length 16384 → 28.5%（OOD 崩溃）\\n- 直接验证：同 checkpoint 前 20 样本，2048→90%，16384→25%\\n\\n### 教训\\n- **eval 时 max_length 必须匹配训练时的值**，否则模型看到 OOD 序列长度会崩溃\\n- full_trace 模型严重依赖训练截断 pattern（不是真正理解 trace 内容）\\n- 这进一步支持 \\\"trace length 不是瓶颈，信号质量才是\\\" 的论点\\n- VOTA 84.45% re-eval 16% 是另一个问题（D032 evaluation artifact，82.6% 垃圾 token），与 max_length 无关\\n\\n### 适用范围\\n- 所有 DeepSeek 实验都需审查 eval max_length 是否匹配训练\\n- Qwen 实验不受影响（Qwen tokenizer 产出更短，大部分不超 2048）\\n\\n## Cluster-Robust CI 决策依据 [2026-05-16]\\n\\n### 发现\\n- 119 problems × 10 samples/problem → within-problem 相关性显著，CR-CI 比 Wilson CI 宽 2.5-3× (中等准确率条件)\\n- 但高准确率条件（ae_only/exception_only >99%）即使用 CR-CI 仍然极窄（<1pp），结论不受影响\\n\\n### 论文策略\\n- **论文中应报告 cluster-robust CI 替代 Wilson CI**，主动回应 \\\"clustered data → inflated significance\\\" 的审稿关切\\n- 这是我们的优势而非弱点：即使用最保守的 CI，核心发现依然稳健\\n- Cluster-robust CI 脚本: `/root/trace-verdict-study/analysis/cluster_robust_ci.py`\\n- 对于 pair-wise comparison (e.g. exception_only vs no_trace)，也应使用 cluster-aware bootstrap (已有 paired bootstrap 结果)\", \"total_chars\": 1189, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```

```json
{
  "project": "trace-verdict-study",
  "file": "memory.md",
  "content": "APPEND_SECTION\n\n## DeepSeek eval max_length 不匹配教训 [2026-05-16]\n\n### 根因\n- 训练 max_length=2048（train_sft.py L164/L193），94.1% 的 full_trace 样本被截断\n- 原始 eval 用默认 max_length=2048 → 79.16%（正确，匹配训练）\n- `run_missing_predictions.sh` 用 --max_length 16384 → 28.5%（OOD 崩溃）\n- 直接验证：同 checkpoint 前 20 样本，2048→90%，16384→25%\n\n### 教训\n- **eval 时 max_length 必须匹配训练时的值**，否则模型看到 OOD 序列长度会崩溃\n- full_trace 模型严重依赖训练截断 pattern（不是真正理解 trace 内容）\n- 这进一步支持 \"trace length 不是瓶颈，信号质量才是\" 的论点\n- VOTA 84.45% re-eval 16% 是另一个问题（D032 evaluation artifact，82.6% 垃圾 token），与 max_length 无关\n\n### 适用范围\n- 所有 DeepSeek 实验都需审查 eval max_length 是否匹配训练\n- Qwen 实验不受影响（Qwen tokenizer 产出更短，大部分不超 2048）\n\n## Cluster-Robust CI 决策依据 [2026-05-16]\n\n### 发现\n- 119 problems × 10 samples/problem → within-problem 相关性显著，CR-CI 比 Wilson CI 宽 2.5-3× (中等准确率条件)\n- 但高准确率条件（ae_only/exception_only >99%）即使用 CR-CI 仍然极窄（<1pp），结论不受影响\n\n### 论文策略\n- **论文中应报告 cluster-robust CI 替代 Wilson CI**，主动回应 \"clustered data → inflated significance\" 的审稿关切\n- 这是我们的优势而非弱点：即使用最保守的 CI，核心发现依然稳健\n- Cluster-robust CI 脚本: `/root/trace-verdict-study/analysis/cluster_robust_ci.py`\n- 对于 pair-wise comparison (e.g. exception_only vs no_trace)，也应使用 cluster-aware bootstrap (已有 paired bootstrap 结果)\n\n## Exception Dominance 跨模型规模不变性 [2026-05-16]\n\n### 发现\n- Qwen-2.5-Coder-1.5B exception_only = 99.83%，与 7B 完全一致（均为 2/1190 错误）\n- 1.5B 训练仅需 29min（7B 约 107min），效率提升 3.7x\n- 三模型验证：Qwen-7B 99.83%, Qwen-1.5B 99.83%, DeepSeek-6.7B 99.33%\n\n### 论文策略\n- 可升级 claim：exception dominance is scale-invariant（至少 1.5B→7B 范围内）\n- 直接回应 \"Do Code Semantics Help?\"（EMNLP 2025 Findings）的负面结论：raw trace 无效，但 exception abstraction 即使对 1.5B 也有效\n- 建议在论文中增加 \"Model Scale Analysis\" 小节或扩展 cross-model table，展示规模无关性\n- 1.5B 的实用部署角度：小模型 + exception trace = 近完美判断，适合 CI/CD 集成"
}
```

[WRITE HANDOFF] 请立即写你的 handoff。

**格式硬约束**:
- 整条回复**只能**是 &lt;handoff>...&lt;/handoff> 标签包裹
- 禁止在标签外写任何寒暄 / 说明 / 确认（"好的"/"我开始写"/"写完了"等）

**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。
  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。
  下一个"你"第一眼看到的就是这里，写错了会被 Director CRITICAL。

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff

示例格式:
&lt;handoff>
### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 cuda:4 / cuda:5（之前两次违规 cuda:0 被 Director CRITICAL）
- submit_training_job 不支持 shell builtin（source/conda activate 全不行）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-8x6000，工作目录 /root/autodl-tmp/hybrid_reasoning_phases
- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python（绝对路径，不走 conda activate）
- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/qwen35_4b_direct
- 活跃：pilot_011 (PID 203221, tmux=pilot_011, cuda:4)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
我刚完成 pilot_010 分析，正在等 pilot_011 (Falcon-H1R) CoT 生成完成（6/50）。
registry: pilot_011 RUNNING；其他全 DONE。

### 4. 📋 待办
- pilot_011 cron 回调 → spawn worker 跑 probe + GC4 计算
- GC4 ≥2pp → 转架构 narrative；<2pp → null-result paper outline 已就绪

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
Falcon-H1R 是最后一个数据点。已知 4B/9B GC4 都 FAIL。
易错：不要把 GC4-par (Falcon parallel) 和 GC4-seq (Qwen sequential) 直接对比。
&lt;/handoff>
